## List 1 definition

> **Approved ONLY when bearing the required kosher symbol. This is the agency's CONDITION OF APPROVAL. It is NOT a claim that any bottle was physically observed carrying the mark.**

**No record in this dataset claims a physical symbol was observed on an actual bottle.** `physical_symbol_independently_observed` is `no_evidence` for every record.
